- **pi-runner**: Hands one task to pi (`pi --mode rpc`, GLM 5.3 Flash by default, DeepSeek V4.1 Flash on request) through `pi-run.sh` and returns its output verbatim — or collects the answer of an already running background job by its job-id, or continues a named session.
